选择器：/* =========================================================
   首页企业优势模块｜恢复卡片展开动效
   说明：
   1. 前面为了修复多语言文字跳动，限制了 transition-property
   2. 企业优势模块的展开动效需要 flex / width / grid 等属性参与过渡
   3. 这里单独恢复该模块的动效，不影响全站文字稳定性
========================================================= */

.home-advantage-panels,
.home-advantage-panel,
.home-advantage-panel::before,
.home-advantage-panel::after,
.home-panel-content,
.home-panel-title,
.home-panel-brief,
.home-panel-detail
{
transition-property:
    flex,
    flex-grow,
    flex-basis,
    width,
    max-width,
    min-width,
    grid-template-columns,
    opacity,
    transform,
    filter,
    background-color,
    color,
    border-color,
    box-shadow !important;

  transition-duration: 0.46s !important;
  transition-timing-function: cubic-bezier(0.22, 1, 0.36, 1) !important;
}
